# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 013 NATURAL DISASTERS EFFECTS
# ============================================================================
# Fresh reusable disaster runtime. Public callers set the documented temp
# variables and optional event targets, then call call_natural_disaster = yes.
# The runtime owns defaults, target resolution, delayed scheduling, impact,
# reports, aftermath, recovery, abnormal paths, and cleanup. Delayed impacts
# never create Event 013 history rows; the sequence call owns the single row.
# ============================================================================

# Duration fields on timed flags require file-scoped literal mirrors. Keep
# these aligned with the Event 013 script-constant tuning table.
@ND_SUPER_EVENT_VISIBLE_DAYS = 14
@ND_IMPACT_GUARD_DAYS = 2
@ND_WARNING_ACTION_LOCK_DAYS = 14
@ND_PARTIAL_RECOVERY_STRAIN_DAYS = 120
@ND_ABNORMAL_NOTICE_COOLDOWN_DAYS = 30

# ============================================================================
# PUBLIC CALL CONTRACT AND EVOLUTION STATE
# ============================================================================

natural_disaster_set_call_defaults = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_result = constant:natural_disaster_call_result.rejected }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_primary_job_count = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_caller_type } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.random }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_caller_event_id } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_event_id = constant:natural_disaster_event.id }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_family } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_family_group } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.random }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_target_mode } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.random_valid }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_target_region } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_region = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_severity } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.local }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.local_season }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_sequence_count } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_count = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_news_policy } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.meaningful }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_report_policy } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.affected_country }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.normal }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_chain_policy } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.family }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_death_scale } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_death_scale = 1.00 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_building_scale } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_damage_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_building_scale = natural_disaster_call_damage_scale }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_building_scale = 1.00 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_warning_scale } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_warning_scale = 1.00 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale = 1.00 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_supply_scale } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_supply_scale = 1.00 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_caller_cost_checked } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_cost_checked = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_caller_cooldown_checked } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_cooldown_checked = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_target_legitimacy_checked } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_legitimacy_checked = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_log_mode } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_log_mode = constant:natural_disaster_log_mode.event_013_history }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_scenario_type } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.random_barrage }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.low }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied = 0 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_validate_call = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type < constant:natural_disaster_caller.random }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type > constant:natural_disaster_caller.max }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_caller }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_event_id < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_caller }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family < constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.max }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_family }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group < constant:natural_disaster_family_group.random }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group > constant:natural_disaster_family_group.max }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_family_group }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group > constant:natural_disaster_family_group.random }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_family_group }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.local }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity > constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_severity }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode < constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.single }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode > constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_sequence_mode }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode < constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.random_valid }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode > constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_target_mode }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_news_policy < constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.meaningful }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_news_policy > constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.none }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_news_policy }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_report_policy < constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.affected_country }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_report_policy > constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.delayed_batch }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_report_policy }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy < constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.none }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy > constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.emergency }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_aftermath_policy }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_chain_policy < constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.none }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_chain_policy > constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.abnormal_path }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_chain_policy }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_log_mode < constant:natural_disaster_log_mode.none }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_log_mode > constant:natural_disaster_log_mode.scenario_history }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_log_mode }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type < constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.random_barrage }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type > constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.full_catalogue }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity < constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.low }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity > constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.maximum }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_scenario }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass > 0 }
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.scenario }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.debug }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_scenario }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_count < 0 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_count > constant:natural_disaster_sequence.maximum_caller_hits }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_sequence_count }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_death_scale < constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.minimum }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_death_scale > constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.maximum }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_building_scale < constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.minimum }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_building_scale > constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.maximum }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_warning_scale < constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.minimum }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_warning_scale > constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.maximum }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale < constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.minimum }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale > constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.maximum }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_supply_scale < constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.minimum }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_supply_scale > constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.maximum }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_scale }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_state }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied < 1 }
				NOT = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_state }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.missing_target }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_country }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied < 1 }
				NOT = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_country }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.missing_target }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_region }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_region < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_region }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
						has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_state
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied > 0 }
						has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_country
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.missing_target }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_state
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_country
			event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						is_owned_by = event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_country
						is_controlled_by = event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_country
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_target }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.deity }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.hostile_actor }
			}
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_cost_checked < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.caller_cost_missing }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.deity }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.hostile_actor }
			}
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_cooldown_checked < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.caller_cooldown_missing }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.deity }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.hostile_actor }
			}
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_legitimacy_checked < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.caller_target_illegitimate }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.abnormal }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal }
			}
			NOT = { natural_disaster_abnormal_family_is_allowed = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.abnormal_locked }
	}
}

natural_disaster_reset_call_inputs = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_event_id = constant:natural_disaster_event.id }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.random_valid }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_region = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.local }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.local_season }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.meaningful }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.affected_country }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.normal }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.family }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_death_scale = 1.00 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_damage_scale = 1.00 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_building_scale = 1.00 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_warning_scale = 1.00 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale = 1.00 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_supply_scale = 1.00 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_cost_checked = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_cooldown_checked = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_legitimacy_checked = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_log_mode = constant:natural_disaster_log_mode.event_013_history }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.random_barrage }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.low }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied = 0 }
}

# Random-event dispatcher preflight. It resolves the first family/state before
# history ownership is accepted, stores the family on that exact state, and
# carries only regular event targets into the canonical entry event.
natural_disaster_prepare_random_event_fire = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_prefire_ready = 0 }
	natural_disaster_set_call_defaults = yes
	natural_disaster_refresh_evolution_state = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
	natural_disaster_resolve_random_family = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.random_valid }
	natural_disaster_resolve_target = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_impact_state
		}
			event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_prefire_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_call_target_state
				OWNER = {
					save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_call_target_country
					save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_log_actor
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_prefire_ready = 1 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_refresh_evolution_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_i_enabled = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_ii_enabled = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_iii_enabled = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage }
	if = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_can_open_evolution_i = yes
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_i_enabled = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage }
	if = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_can_open_evolution_ii = yes
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_ii_enabled = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage }
	if = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_can_open_evolution_iii = yes
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_iii_enabled = 1 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_record_reached_evolutions = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_i_enabled > 0 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass > 0 } }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_evolution_i_logged }
		}
		set_global_flag = natural_disaster_evolution_i_logged
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:natural_disaster_event.id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_type }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_tier }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_log_actor }
			event_target:natural_disaster_log_actor = { save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor }
		}
		else = { save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor }
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_ii_enabled > 0 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass > 0 } }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_evolution_ii_logged }
		}
		set_global_flag = natural_disaster_evolution_ii_logged
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:natural_disaster_event.id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_type }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_tier }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_log_actor }
			event_target:natural_disaster_log_actor = { save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor }
		}
		else = { save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor }
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 2 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_iii_enabled > 0 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass > 0 } }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_evolution_iii_logged }
		}
		set_global_flag = natural_disaster_evolution_iii_logged
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:natural_disaster_event.id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_type }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_tier }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_log_actor }
			event_target:natural_disaster_log_actor = { save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor }
		}
		else = { save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor }
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
	}
	natural_disaster_maybe_emit_abnormal_age = yes
}

natural_disaster_record_call_history = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_log_mode = constant:natural_disaster_log_mode.event_013_history }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_log_mode = constant:natural_disaster_log_mode.scenario_history }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.last_fired_event_id = constant:natural_disaster_event.id }
		record_events_log_history_entry = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_emit_super_event = {
	clear_global_event_target = natural_disaster_super_event_scope
	save_global_event_target_as = natural_disaster_super_event_scope
	set_global_flag = {
		flag = super_event_visible
		days = @ND_SUPER_EVENT_VISIBLE_DAYS
		value = natural_disaster_super_event_slot
	}
	set_variable = { global.current_super_event_audio_id = natural_disaster_super_event_audio_id }
	every_country = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		play_current_super_event_audio = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_maybe_emit_abnormal_age = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 2 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass > 0 } }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_abnormal_age_fired }
		}
		set_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_abnormal_age_fired
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_reveal_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_slot = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.super_event_abnormal_disaster_age }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_audio_id = constant:natural_disaster_super_event_audio.abnormal_disaster_age }
		natural_disaster_emit_super_event = yes
	}
}

# State scope, after the impact is confirmed. Family milestones are once-only
# and are suppressed for the same sequence that first revealed Evolution III.
natural_disaster_maybe_emit_family_super_event = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_suppressed = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_reveal_sequence_id
			check_variable = {
				var = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_reveal_sequence_id
				value = natural_disaster_sequence_id
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_suppressed = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_suppressed < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_earth_rupture_fired }
			}
			set_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_earth_rupture_fired
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_slot = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.super_event_great_rupture }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_audio_id = constant:natural_disaster_super_event_audio.earth_rupture }
			natural_disaster_emit_super_event = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_massive_eruption_fired }
			}
			set_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_massive_eruption_fired
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_slot = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.super_event_massive_eruption }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_audio_id = constant:natural_disaster_super_event_audio.massive_eruption }
			natural_disaster_emit_super_event = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				}
				NOT = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_skyfall_fired }
			}
			set_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_skyfall_fired
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_slot = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.super_event_skyfall }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_audio_id = constant:natural_disaster_super_event_audio.skyfall }
			natural_disaster_emit_super_event = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
				}
				NOT = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_storm_corridor_fired }
			}
			set_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_storm_corridor_fired
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_slot = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.super_event_storm_corridor }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_audio_id = constant:natural_disaster_super_event_audio.storm_corridor }
			natural_disaster_emit_super_event = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_origin_qualifies
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_tsunami_coastal_groups
					value = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.delayed_tsunami_min_coastal_groups
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_sequence_planned_hits
					value = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.large_tsunami_path_hits
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				NOT = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_delayed_tsunami_fired }
			}
			set_global_flag = natural_disaster_super_event_delayed_tsunami_fired
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_slot = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.super_event_delayed_tsunami_chain }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_super_event_audio_id = constant:natural_disaster_super_event_audio.delayed_tsunami_chain }
			natural_disaster_emit_super_event = yes
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. Selects one live aftermath state for relief, GUI focus, and
# other callers that need a deterministic urgency target. Optional input:
# natural_disaster_priority_variant (natural_disaster_relief_variant) and
# natural_disaster_priority_phase (zero for any open card).
natural_disaster_select_priority_open_card = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_best_score = -1 }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_priority_variant } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_variant = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_priority_phase } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_phase = 0 }
	}
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory }
			natural_disaster_state_matches_priority_phase = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = state_population_k }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = natural_disaster_priority_score
			min = 0
			max = constant:natural_disaster_priority.population_cap
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_severity_score = natural_disaster_severity }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_severity_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.severity_weight }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = natural_disaster_priority_severity_score }
		if = {
			limit = { is_capital = yes }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.capital_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.transport_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.industry_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_coastal = yes }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.coast_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.chain_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_variant = constant:natural_disaster_relief_variant.port_lifeline }
				is_coastal = yes
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.variant_match_bonus }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_variant = constant:natural_disaster_relief_variant.engineer }
				OR = {
					natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes
					natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.variant_match_bonus }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_variant = constant:natural_disaster_relief_variant.medical }
				OR = {
					natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_disease_pressure
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.variant_match_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score > natural_disaster_priority_best_score } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_best_score = natural_disaster_priority_score }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected = 1 }
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_priority_open_card_state
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_resolve_call_severity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.random }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.cluster }
			}
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.local }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_i_severe_chance = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_i_severe_chance }
			random = {
				chance = natural_disaster_evolution_i_severe_chance
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal }
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_resolve_sequence_count = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_count > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = natural_disaster_call_sequence_count }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.single } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.maximum } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.barrage_maximum_hits }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.high } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.barrage_high_hits }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.medium } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.barrage_medium_hits }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.barrage_low_hits }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage } }
		set_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_sequence_hits
			min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_iii_min_hits
			max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_iii_max_hits
			integer = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage } }
		set_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_sequence_hits
			min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_ii_min_hits
			max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_ii_max_hits
			integer = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
		set_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_sequence_hits
			min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_i_min_hits
			max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_i_max_hits
			integer = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_sequence_hits
			min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.baseline_min_hits
			max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.baseline_max_hits
			integer = yes
		}
	}
	round_temp_variable = natural_disaster_sequence_hits
}

# ============================================================================
# PERSISTENT DELAYED JOB QUEUE
# ============================================================================

natural_disaster_reserve_job_day = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date = natural_disaster_job_desired_due_date }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_day_conflict = 1 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_day_conflict > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_day_conflict = 0 }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries^num
			value = i
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries^i
						value = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = global.natural_disaster_reserved_day_entries^i
						value = natural_disaster_job_due_date
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_day_conflict = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_day_conflict > 0 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date = 1 }
		}
	}
	add_to_array = {
		array = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries
		value = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id
	}
	add_to_array = {
		array = global.natural_disaster_reserved_day_entries
		value = natural_disaster_job_due_date
	}
}

natural_disaster_release_job_day = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reservation_index = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries^num
		value = i
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_reservation_index < 0 }
				check_variable = {
					var = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries^i
					value = natural_disaster_current_job_sequence_id
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = global.natural_disaster_reserved_day_entries^i
					value = natural_disaster_current_job_due_date
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reservation_index = i }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_reservation_index > constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index } }
		remove_from_array = {
			array = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries
			index = natural_disaster_reservation_index
		}
		remove_from_array = {
			array = global.natural_disaster_reserved_day_entries
			index = natural_disaster_reservation_index
		}
	}
}

# State scope. Inputs are natural_disaster_job_type and
# natural_disaster_job_delay_days. The state owns persistent disaster payload;
# its current controller owns only the delayed queue row and worker wakeup.
natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_desired_due_date = global.date }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_desired_due_date = natural_disaster_job_delay_days }
	natural_disaster_reserve_job_day = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_effective_delay = natural_disaster_job_due_date }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_effective_delay = global.date }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = natural_disaster_job_effective_delay
		min = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.minimum_job_delay_days
		max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.season_expiry_days
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_next_subevent_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date }
	controller = {
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries
			value = PREV
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_type_entries
			value = natural_disaster_job_type
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries
			value = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries
			value = natural_disaster_job_due_date
		}
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_queue_active
		country_event = {
			id = chaosx.nr13.2
			days = natural_disaster_job_effective_delay
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_execute_warning = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
			}
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.warning }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.warning }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.warning }
			controller = {
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_unread
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_fire_family_report = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_family }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = natural_disaster_severity }
		natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = yes
		save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_report_state
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_report_policy = natural_disaster_report_policy }
		controller = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.affected_country }
						check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.delayed_batch }
					}
				}
				natural_disaster_achievement_record_report_group = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.global } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_global_announcer_used
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.caller } }
			var:natural_disaster_caller_country = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.[REPORT_EVENT_ID] } }
					REPORT_EVENT_ID = "[?natural_disaster_report_event_id|.0]"
				}
			}
			controller = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = PREV.natural_disaster_caller_country
							value = THIS
							compare = not_equals
						}
					}
					meta_effect = {
						text = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.[REPORT_EVENT_ID] } }
						REPORT_EVENT_ID = "[?natural_disaster_report_event_id|.0]"
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.global } }
			every_country = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.[REPORT_EVENT_ID] } }
					REPORT_EVENT_ID = "[?natural_disaster_report_event_id|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			controller = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.[REPORT_EVENT_ID] } }
					REPORT_EVENT_ID = "[?natural_disaster_report_event_id|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_fire_family_news = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_family }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = natural_disaster_severity }
		natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = yes
		save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_news_state
		controller = {
			meta_effect = {
				text = { news_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.[NEWS_EVENT_ID] } }
				NEWS_EVENT_ID = "[?natural_disaster_news_event_id|.0]"
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_process_due_job = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_index = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_best_due_date = global.date }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^num
		value = i
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^i
					value = global.date
					compare = less_than_or_equals
				}
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_index < 0 }
					check_variable = {
						var = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^i
						value = natural_disaster_job_best_due_date
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_index = i }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_best_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^i }
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_index > constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = natural_disaster_job_type_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_sequence_id = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		var:natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries^natural_disaster_job_index = {
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_type_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		natural_disaster_release_job_day = yes

		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_impact_state }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.warning } }
				natural_disaster_execute_warning = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.impact } }
				natural_disaster_execute_impact = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.report } }
				natural_disaster_fire_family_report = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.news } }
				natural_disaster_fire_family_news = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.followup } }
				natural_disaster_execute_chain_followup = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.reassessment } }
				natural_disaster_execute_reassessment = yes
			}
		}
	}
	natural_disaster_achievement_mark_sequence_ended = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^num < 1 } }
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_queue_active
	}
}

# ============================================================================
# FAMILY AND TARGET RESOLUTION
# ============================================================================

natural_disaster_resolve_random_family = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group > constant:natural_disaster_family_group.random }
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.seismic } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.water_coastal } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
				random_list = {
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 } }
				random_list = {
					50 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.fire_drought } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 } }
				random_list = {
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
					50 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.winter_temperature } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 } }
				random_list = {
					45 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					55 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
					45 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.storm_weather } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
				random_list = {
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 } }
				random_list = {
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.mass_movement } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 } }
				random_list = {
					50 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
					50 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.volcanic_ash } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
				random_list = {
					45 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.dust_sand } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm }
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
				25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
				25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
				25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.geological_crisis }
		}
		random_list = {
			18 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
			15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
			4 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.weather_crisis }
		}
		random_list = {
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
			6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
			4 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.skyfall_crisis }
		}
		random_list = {
			25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
			40 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.full_catalogue }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.maximum } }
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_current_family
				min = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake
				max = constant:natural_disaster_family.max
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_current_family
				min = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake
				max = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact
				integer = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_force_ordinary_pool > 0 }
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
			6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
			6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage } }
		random_list = {
			6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			4 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
			4 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
			4 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
			11 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage } }
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
			6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
			6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
		random_list = {
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
			9 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
			9 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
			9 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
			9 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_list = {
			16 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			16 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			11 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
			9 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
		}
	}

	# Non-maximum manual barrages never draw abnormal families. Re-resolve
	# inside the selected crisis family instead of substituting a skyfall hit.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity < constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.maximum }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.geological_crisis } }
			random_list = {
				22 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
				16 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
				16 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
				20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
				12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
				14 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.weather_crisis } }
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_current_family
				min = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood
				max = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.skyfall_crisis } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable_to_random = {
