				var = natural_disaster_current_family
				min = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake
				max = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact
				integer = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_resolve_family = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index > 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
				random_list = {
					55 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
				random_list = {
					45 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
				random_list = {
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
				random_list = {
					65 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
				random_list = {
					65 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_call_family }
	}
	else = {
		natural_disaster_resolve_random_family = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
			NOT = { natural_disaster_abnormal_family_is_allowed = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
	}
}

natural_disaster_score_family_target_state = {
	set_temp_variable_to_random = {
		var = natural_disaster_target_candidate_score
		min = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.random_min
		max = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.random_max
		integer = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
		if = { limit = { is_capital = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.capital } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_wet_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.wet_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_supply_node = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.supply_node } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_naval_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.naval_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { is_capital = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.capital } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.low_infrastructure } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_wet_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.wet_hazard } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
	}

		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.unresolved_aftermath }
	}

natural_disaster_select_suitable_state_from_target_country = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_best_score = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index }
	event_target:natural_disaster_target_country = {
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_controlled_by = PREV
				natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
			}
			natural_disaster_score_family_target_state = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score > natural_disaster_target_best_score } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_best_score = natural_disaster_target_candidate_score }
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_target_country
			event_target:natural_disaster_target_country = {
				any_owned_state = {
					is_controlled_by = PREV
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
				}
			}
		}
		natural_disaster_select_suitable_state_from_target_country = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_resolve_target = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_previous_path_state
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required > 0 }
			event_target:natural_disaster_previous_path_state = {
				any_neighbor_state = { natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes }
			}
		}
			event_target:natural_disaster_previous_path_state = {
				random_neighbor_state = {
					limit = { natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes }
					save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
					controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_state }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_state
			event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
				OR = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_is_valid_repeat_impact_state = yes
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
			controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_country }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_country
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_country = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
		natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_state }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_target_country
		}
		natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_region }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_region > 0 }
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				random_state = {
					limit = {
						region = [REGION_ID]
						natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					}
					save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
					controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
				}
			}
			REGION_ID = "[?natural_disaster_call_target_region|.0]"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_state
			event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
				OR = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_is_valid_repeat_impact_state = yes
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
			controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_target_country
		}
		natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_country
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_country = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
		natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.nearby_enemy }
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				has_war_with = ROOT
					any_owned_state = {
						is_controlled_by = PREV
						natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					}
			}
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country
		}
		natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.coast }
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
					any_owned_state = {
						is_controlled_by = PREV
						natural_disaster_is_valid_coastal_impact_state = yes
					}
			}
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { natural_disaster_is_valid_coastal_impact_state = yes }
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.dense_state }
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
					any_owned_state = {
						is_controlled_by = PREV
						natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 } }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
					any_owned_state = {
						is_controlled_by = PREV
						natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					}
			}
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country
		}
		natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_impact_state
			event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
				OR = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_is_valid_repeat_impact_state = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved = 1 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_set_next_gap = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 } }
		set_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_next_gap_days
			min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.first_impact_min_days
			max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.first_impact_max_days
			integer = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage } }
		set_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_next_gap_days
			min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_iii_min_gap_days
			max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_iii_max_gap_days
			integer = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage } }
		set_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_next_gap_days
			min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_ii_min_gap_days
			max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_ii_max_gap_days
			integer = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
		set_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_next_gap_days
			min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_i_min_gap_days
			max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_i_max_gap_days
			integer = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_next_gap_days
			min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.baseline_min_gap_days
			max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.baseline_max_gap_days
			integer = yes
		}
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_schedule_delay = natural_disaster_next_gap_days }
}

natural_disaster_configure_scheduled_state = {
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_rescue_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilization_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_arrived
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_prevented
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_terminal_failure_consequence
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftershock_active
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_aftermath
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_lahar_aftermath
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_wildfire_spread
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_famine_pressure
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_disease_pressure
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_pressure
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_supply_collapse
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_arrived
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_relief_misdirected
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_relief_refused
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_impact_index = natural_disaster_sequence_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = natural_disaster_planned_origin_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_planned_hits = natural_disaster_sequence_hits }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_segment_index = natural_disaster_sequence_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.forecast }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_confidence = natural_disaster_warning_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_remaining_segments = natural_disaster_sequence_hits }
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_path_remaining_segments = natural_disaster_sequence_index }
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_path_remaining_segments = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_previous_path_state }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_linked_state = event_target:natural_disaster_previous_path_state }
	}
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_origin_qualifies
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
			is_coastal = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
			}
		}
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_origin_qualifies
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_tsunami_coastal_groups < 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_coastal_groups = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_last_coast_country = OWNER }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = nd_tsunami_last_coast_country value = OWNER compare = not_equals }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_coastal_groups = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_last_coast_country = OWNER }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_sequence_index
					value = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.delayed_tsunami_min_segment_index
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_call_severity
					value = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.delayed_tsunami_min_severity
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival = 1 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_tsunami_coastal_groups = nd_tsunami_coastal_groups }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival > 0 } }
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival
		}
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = natural_disaster_call_severity }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution = natural_disaster_current_evolution }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = natural_disaster_call_news_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = natural_disaster_call_report_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = natural_disaster_call_chain_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_death_scale = natural_disaster_call_death_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_building_scale = natural_disaster_call_building_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_scale = natural_disaster_call_warning_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_supply_scale = natural_disaster_call_supply_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.light } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = constant:natural_disaster_recovery_scale.light }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.full } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = constant:natural_disaster_recovery_scale.full }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.emergency } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = constant:natural_disaster_recovery_scale.emergency }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_type = natural_disaster_call_caller_type }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_event_id = natural_disaster_call_caller_event_id }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_country = ROOT }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_schedule_date = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_schedule_date = natural_disaster_schedule_delay }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.warning }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.warning }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_preparation_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = 1.00 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = 2.00 }
	round_temp_variable = natural_disaster_recovery_burden
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = natural_disaster_recovery_burden }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = natural_disaster_recovery_burden }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = natural_disaster_recovery_burden }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 1 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_ii_score_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_ii_score_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_ii_score_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 2 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_iii_score_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_iii_score_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_iii_score_penalty }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_cleanup_state = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_action_result = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.none }
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
	set_state_flag = {
		flag = natural_disaster_impact_guard
		days = @ND_IMPACT_GUARD_DAYS
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 } }
		OWNER = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_log_actor }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	OWNER = { natural_disaster_achievement_begin_sequence = yes }
	natural_disaster_register_abnormal_gui_card = yes
}

natural_disaster_configure_repeated_impact = {
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_impact_index = natural_disaster_sequence_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_origin_family = natural_disaster_planned_origin_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_planned_hits = natural_disaster_sequence_hits }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_severity = natural_disaster_call_severity }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_evolution = natural_disaster_current_evolution }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_news_policy = natural_disaster_call_news_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_report_policy = natural_disaster_call_report_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_chain_policy = natural_disaster_call_chain_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_death_scale = natural_disaster_call_death_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_building_scale = natural_disaster_call_building_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_supply_scale = natural_disaster_call_supply_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_caller_country = ROOT }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_schedule_date = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_schedule_date = natural_disaster_schedule_delay }
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_repeat_impact_scheduled
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
	set_state_flag = { flag = natural_disaster_impact_guard days = @ND_IMPACT_GUARD_DAYS }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_id = natural_disaster_repeat_sequence_id }
	OWNER = { natural_disaster_achievement_begin_sequence = yes }
}

natural_disaster_schedule_warning_and_impact = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_scheduled = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes }
			natural_disaster_configure_repeated_impact = yes
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_scheduled = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			natural_disaster_configure_scheduled_state = yes
		}

		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_warning.base_chance }
		if = {
			limit = { infrastructure > constant:natural_disaster_threshold.strategic_infrastructure }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_warning.good_infrastructure_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { radar_station > 0 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_warning.radar_bonus }
		}
		OWNER = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_war = yes }
				add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_warning.war_penalty }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_stability < constant:natural_disaster_factor.low_stability_threshold }
				add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_warning.low_stability_penalty }
			}
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = natural_disaster_call_warning_scale }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = natural_disaster_warning_percent
			min = constant:natural_disaster_warning.minimum_chance
			max = constant:natural_disaster_warning.maximum_chance
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_factor.percent_to_random_chance }
		round_temp_variable = natural_disaster_warning_percent

		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_delay = natural_disaster_schedule_delay }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_delay = 1 }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = natural_disaster_warning_delay
			min = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.minimum_job_delay_days
			max = natural_disaster_schedule_delay
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_scheduled < 1 } }
			random = {
				chance = natural_disaster_warning_percent
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.warning }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_warning_delay }
				natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.impact }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_schedule_delay }
		natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_plan_sequence = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_schedule_delay = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_scheduled_hits = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_skipped_hits = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_force_ordinary_pool = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_coastal_groups = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_last_coast_country = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage } }
		}
		random_list = {
			20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
			20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
			20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
			20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.moving_corridor }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = natural_disaster_call_family }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family }

	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index < natural_disaster_sequence_hits } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_iii_abnormal_path_hits }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_force_ordinary_pool = 1 }
		}
		natural_disaster_resolve_family = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family < 1 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family < 1 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
		}
		natural_disaster_resolve_target = yes
		natural_disaster_set_next_gap = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved > 0 } }
			natural_disaster_schedule_warning_and_impact = yes
			event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_previous_path_state }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_scheduled_hits = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_skipped_hits = 1 }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 1 }
	}

}

natural_disaster_call = {
	natural_disaster_set_call_defaults = yes
	natural_disaster_validate_call = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid > 0 } }
		natural_disaster_refresh_evolution_state = yes
		natural_disaster_resolve_call_severity = yes
		natural_disaster_resolve_sequence_count = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id_existed = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_previous_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id_existed = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_previous_sequence_id = 0 }
			set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_sequence_id = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.natural_disaster_sequence_id = 1 }
		natural_disaster_plan_sequence = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_scheduled_hits > 0 } }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_last_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_last_sequence_planned_hits = natural_disaster_sequence_hits }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_last_sequence_scheduled_hits = natural_disaster_scheduled_hits }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_last_sequence_skipped_hits = natural_disaster_skipped_hits }
			set_country_flag = natural_disaster_sequence_anchor
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_result = constant:natural_disaster_call_result.accepted }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_primary_job_count = natural_disaster_scheduled_hits }
			natural_disaster_record_reached_evolutions = yes
			natural_disaster_record_call_history = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.no_eligible_target }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id_existed > 0 } }
				set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_sequence_id = natural_disaster_previous_sequence_id }
			}
			else = {
				clear_variable = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id
			}
		}
	}
}

# FAMILY PROFILES AND IMPACT RESOLUTION
# ============================================================================

natural_disaster_apply_resilience_profile = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_death_mult = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 0 }

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_resilient_rails
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport > 0 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.general_transport_damage_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.general_transport_damage_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_death_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.secondary_death_mult }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_seismic_retrofit
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_coastal_barriers
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_firebreak_network
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_volcanic_exclusion_routes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_water_security
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_exclusion_cordon
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_weather_shelter_network
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_slope_stabilization
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_death_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.related_death_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.related_building_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.related_disruption_mult }
	}
}

		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.volcanic_ash }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_primary_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.population }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_secondary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_tertiary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.volcanic_ash }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }

	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_primary_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.transport }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_secondary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_tertiary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.water_coastal }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_primary_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.population }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_secondary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_tertiary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.storm_weather }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_primary_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.population }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_secondary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_tertiary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.abnormal }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_primary_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.transport }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_secondary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_tertiary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.abnormal }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_primary_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.food_water }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_secondary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_tertiary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.abnormal }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_primary_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.transport }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_secondary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_tertiary_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.abnormal }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_action_taken
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.none }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.primary }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.primary }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.none }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = natural_disaster_warning_primary_protection }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = natural_disaster_resolved_family_group }
}

natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_fraction = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.abnormal }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.abnormal_points }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_days = constant:natural_disaster_damage.abnormal_duration_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_supply }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_supply_impact }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_speed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_attrition }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_repair }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_resources }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic } }
