		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.catastrophic_supply }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.catastrophic_supply_impact }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.catastrophic_speed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.catastrophic_attrition }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.catastrophic_repair }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.catastrophic_resources }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_fraction = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.regional }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.regional_points }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_days = constant:natural_disaster_damage.regional_duration_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.regional_supply }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.regional_supply_impact }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.regional_speed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.regional_attrition }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.regional_repair }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.regional_resources }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_fraction = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.severe }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.severe_points }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_days = constant:natural_disaster_damage.severe_duration_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.severe_supply }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.severe_supply_impact }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.severe_speed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.severe_attrition }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.severe_repair }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.severe_resources }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_fraction = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.local }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.local_points }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_days = constant:natural_disaster_damage.local_duration_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.local_supply }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.local_supply_impact }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.local_speed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.local_attrition }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.local_repair }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.local_resources }
	}
}

natural_disaster_damage_selected_building = {
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			damage_building = {
				type = [BUILDING_TYPE]
				damage = [BUILDING_DAMAGE]
			}
		}
		BUILDING_TYPE = "[?natural_disaster_damage_building.GetTokenKey]"
		BUILDING_DAMAGE = "[?natural_disaster_building_damage|2]"
	}
}

natural_disaster_apply_family_building_damage = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = natural_disaster_building_scale }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = nd_effect_building_scale
			check_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = nd_effect_building_scale }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = constant:natural_disaster_factor.warning_building_damage }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_preparation_score > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = constant:natural_disaster_factor.prepared_building_damage }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.transport }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.industry_air }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.coast }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.fire }
			}
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = constant:natural_disaster_warning.material_protection_mult }
	}
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_damage = natural_disaster_damage_points }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_damage = nd_resolved_building_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_building = natural_disaster_primary_building }
	natural_disaster_damage_selected_building = yes

	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_damage = constant:natural_disaster_factor.secondary_building_damage }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_building = natural_disaster_secondary_building }
	natural_disaster_damage_selected_building = yes

	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_damage = constant:natural_disaster_factor.tertiary_from_secondary_damage }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_building = natural_disaster_tertiary_building }
	natural_disaster_damage_selected_building = yes

	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_points = natural_disaster_damage_points }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_transport = natural_disaster_damage_transport }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_industry = natural_disaster_damage_industry }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_ports = natural_disaster_damage_ports }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_air = natural_disaster_damage_air }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_resources = natural_disaster_damage_resources }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_shelter = natural_disaster_damage_shelter }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = nd_card_damage_points }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport = nd_card_damage_transport }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_industry = nd_card_damage_industry }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_ports = nd_card_damage_ports }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_air = nd_card_damage_air }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_resources = nd_card_damage_resources }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_shelter = nd_card_damage_shelter }
}

natural_disaster_apply_population_loss = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_death_scale = natural_disaster_death_scale }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = nd_effect_death_scale
			check_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_death_scale = nd_effect_death_scale }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = natural_disaster_family_lethality }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = nd_resolved_death_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = natural_disaster_resilience_death_mult }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.evolution_ii_mult }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 2 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.evolution_iii_mult }
	}
	OWNER = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_war = yes }
			multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.war_mult }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_stability < constant:natural_disaster_factor.low_stability_threshold }
			multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.low_stability_mult }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.unresolved_aftermath_mult }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.warning_success_mult }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_preparation_score > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.prepared_mult }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.population }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.food_water }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.coast }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.medical }
			}
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_warning.population_protection_mult }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.local_cap_factor }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.severe_cap_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.regional_cap_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.catastrophic_cap_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.abnormal_cap_factor }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_population_cap = state_population_k }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_population_cap = constant:natural_disaster_factor.population_thousands_to_people }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_population_cap = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.maximum_state_fraction }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_floor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.minimum_people }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_floor > natural_disaster_population_cap } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_floor = natural_disaster_population_cap }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_change = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_percent = natural_disaster_death_fraction }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_mult = natural_disaster_death_multiplier }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_cap_factor = natural_disaster_death_cap_factor }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_cap_floor = natural_disaster_death_cap_floor }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_cap_hard_max = natural_disaster_population_cap }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_exact_percent = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.natural_disaster }
	chaos_meter_register_state_civilian_deaths_percent = yes
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_last_deaths = chaos_deaths_change }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_total_deaths = chaos_deaths_change }
}

natural_disaster_apply_state_disruption = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply = natural_disaster_mod_supply }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply_impact = natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_speed = natural_disaster_mod_speed }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_attrition = natural_disaster_mod_attrition }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_repair = natural_disaster_mod_repair }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_resources = natural_disaster_mod_resources }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_ports < 1 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.incidental_system_factor }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.incidental_system_factor }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.secondary_system_factor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_shelter < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_air < 1 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.secondary_system_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_shelter < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_air > 0 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.airfield_attrition_factor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_industry < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_ports < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_air < 1 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.incidental_system_factor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_resources < 1 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.incidental_system_factor }
	}
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply = natural_disaster_supply_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply_impact = natural_disaster_supply_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply_impact = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_speed = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_attrition = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_repair = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_resources = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_days = natural_disaster_modifier_days }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = nd_state_mod_supply }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = nd_state_mod_supply_impact }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = nd_state_mod_speed }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = nd_state_mod_attrition }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = nd_state_mod_repair }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = nd_state_mod_resources }
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_state_disruption }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_expiry = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_expiry = nd_state_mod_days }
}

natural_disaster_resolve_chain_risk = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.famine } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.disease } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.refugee } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.infrastructure } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.tsunami } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.aftershock } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.family }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.abnormal_path }
			}
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.secondary }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_secondary_chain > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = natural_disaster_warning_secondary_chain }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.family }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.abnormal_path }
			}
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.tertiary }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_tertiary_chain > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = natural_disaster_warning_tertiary_chain }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.family }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.abnormal_path }
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_current_severity
				value = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			is_coastal = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = natural_disaster_default_chain }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_geological_tsunami_chance = constant:natural_disaster_chain_weight.geological_tsunami }
		random = {
			chance = natural_disaster_geological_tsunami_chance
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.none } }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity > constant:natural_disaster_severity.local }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = natural_disaster_default_chain }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = natural_disaster_resolved_chain }
}

natural_disaster_pay_warning_field_teams = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_manpower }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = -1 }
	add_manpower = nd_warning_manpower_cost
	if = {
		limit = { has_equipment = { support_equipment_1 > constant:natural_disaster_cost_gate.warning_support_equipment } }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_equipment_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_support_equipment }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_equipment_cost = -1 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_warning_equipment_cost }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_equipment_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_trucks }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_equipment_cost = -1 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_warning_equipment_cost }
	}
	add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.warning_stability
}

natural_disaster_pay_warning_transport = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_manpower }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = -1 }
	add_manpower = nd_warning_manpower_cost
	if = {
		limit = { has_equipment = { train_equipment_1 > constant:natural_disaster_cost_gate.warning_trains } }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_transport_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_trains }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_transport_cost = -1 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = nd_warning_transport_cost }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_transport_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_convoys }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_transport_cost = -1 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = nd_warning_transport_cost }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_fuel }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_fuel_cost = -1 }
	add_fuel = nd_warning_fuel_cost
	add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.warning_stability
}

natural_disaster_pay_warning_shelter = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_manpower }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = -1 }
	add_manpower = nd_warning_manpower_cost
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_support_equipment }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_support_cost = -1 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_warning_support_cost }
	add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.warning_stability
}

natural_disaster_apply_warning_direction = {
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = nd_warning_choice }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = nd_warning_protection }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_action_result = nd_warning_choice }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_preparation_score = constant:natural_disaster_warning.preparation_score }
	set_state_flag = { flag = natural_disaster_warning_action_taken days = @ND_WARNING_ACTION_LOCK_DAYS }
}

natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = {
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.base_rescue_cap }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.base_stabilization_cap }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.base_reconstruction_cap }
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_major = no
			num_of_factories < constant:natural_disaster_threshold.weak_country_factory_count
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.weak_country_cap }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.weak_country_cap }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.minimum_active_cap }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.weak_country_reconstruction_cap }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_major = yes }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.major_country_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.major_country_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.major_country_bonus_cap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.foreign_relief_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.foreign_relief_bonus_cap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_war = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.war_cap_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.war_cap_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.war_cap_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_pressure_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_pressure_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_pressure_bonus_cap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_ended }
		}
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.barrage_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.barrage_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.barrage_bonus_cap }
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = natural_disaster_rescue_cap
		min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.minimum_active_cap
		max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_rescue_cap
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = natural_disaster_stabilization_cap
		min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.minimum_active_cap
		max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_stabilization_cap
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap
		min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.minimum_active_cap
		max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_reconstruction_cap
	}
}

natural_disaster_activate_rescue_mission_for_current_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_candidate_state = PREV }
	natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 0 }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_1 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 1 }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_2 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 2 }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_3 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 3 }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_4 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4
	}
}

natural_disaster_activate_stabilization_mission_for_current_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_candidate_state = PREV }
	natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = yes
	if = {

}

natural_disaster_activate_reconstruction_mission_for_current_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_candidate_state = PREV }
	natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = yes
	if = {
		limit = {

	}

		activate_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_3
	}
}

natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = {
	natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration < constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_rescue_cap } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue }
		natural_disaster_select_priority_open_card = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected > 0 } }
				event_target:natural_disaster_priority_open_card_state = {
					controller = { natural_disaster_activate_rescue_mission_for_current_state = yes }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_rescue_cap } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration < constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_stabilization_cap } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.middle_stabilization }
		natural_disaster_select_priority_open_card = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected > 0 } }
				event_target:natural_disaster_priority_open_card_state = {
					controller = { natural_disaster_activate_stabilization_mission_for_current_state = yes }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_stabilization_cap } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration < constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_reconstruction_cap } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.late_reconstruction }
		natural_disaster_select_priority_open_card = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected > 0 } }
				event_target:natural_disaster_priority_open_card_state = {
					controller = { natural_disaster_activate_reconstruction_mission_for_current_state = yes }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_reconstruction_cap } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 1 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_activate_family_chain_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_active }
			PREV = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_active
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_state = PREV }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension = PREV.natural_disaster_chain_due_date }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension = global.date }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_mission_deadline_offset_days }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension < 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_pay_chain_prevention = {
	controller = {
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_manpower }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = -1 }
			add_manpower = nd_chain_manpower_cost
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_convoy_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_convoys }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_convoy_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = nd_chain_convoy_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_fuel }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = -1 }
			add_fuel = nd_chain_fuel_cost
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.rescue_stability
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_manpower }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = -1 }
			add_manpower = nd_chain_manpower_cost
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_support_equipment }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_support_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.rescue_stability
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_manpower }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = -1 }
			add_manpower = nd_chain_manpower_cost
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_support_equipment }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_support_cost }
			if = {
				limit = { has_equipment = { train_equipment_1 > constant:natural_disaster_cost_gate.stabilize_trains } }
				set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_trains }
				multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_transport_cost }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_convoys }
				multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = nd_chain_transport_cost }
			}
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.stabilize_stability
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_manpower }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = -1 }
			add_manpower = nd_chain_manpower_cost
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_support_equipment }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_support_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_fuel }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = -1 }
			add_fuel = nd_chain_fuel_cost
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.rescue_stability
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_support_equipment }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_support_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			if = {
				limit = { has_equipment = { train_equipment_1 > constant:natural_disaster_cost_gate.stabilize_trains } }
				set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_trains }
				multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_transport_cost }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_convoys }
				multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = nd_chain_transport_cost }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_fuel }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = -1 }
			add_fuel = nd_chain_fuel_cost
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.stabilize_stability
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_manpower }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = -1 }
			add_manpower = nd_chain_manpower_cost
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_train_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_trains }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_train_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_train_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_fuel }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = -1 }
			add_fuel = nd_chain_fuel_cost
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.rescue_stability
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_support_equipment }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_support_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_train_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_trains }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_train_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_train_cost }
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.stabilize_stability
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_apply_chain_prevention_work = {
	natural_disaster_achievement_record_chain_prevention = yes
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_prevention_score }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue } }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = constant:natural_disaster_warning.preparation_score }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = constant:natural_disaster_warning.preparation_score }
	}
}

# State scope. Removes only the country mission slots targeting this card.
natural_disaster_release_state_mission_slots = {
	controller = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_rescue_state_1 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_rescue_state_2 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_rescue_state_3 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_rescue_state_4 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_4
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_1
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_1 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_1
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_2
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_2 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_2
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_3
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_3 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_3
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_1
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_1 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_1
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_2
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_2 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_2
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_3
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_3 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_3
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_3
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_release_special_state_missions = {
	controller = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_mission }
			clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_active
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_mission }
			clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active
			clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_secured
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_donor
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = {
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_next_reassessment = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_next_reassessment = natural_disaster_reassessment_delay }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.reassessment }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_reassessment_delay }
	natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
}

natural_disaster_refresh_country_notification_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active }
			NOT = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
						natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_unread
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
	}
}

natural_disaster_close_aftermath_card = {
	natural_disaster_achievement_track_card_close = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_close_with_partial_strain = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_closed_abnormal_card = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_close_with_partial_strain = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_card }
		clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_card
		clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_1
		clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_2
		clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_3
		clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_4
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
		clear_variable = achievement_nd_refugee_counted_sequence
		clear_variable = achievement_nd_refugee_recovered_sequence
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_gui_card }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_closed_abnormal_card = 1 }
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_gui_card
		controller = {
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_abnormal_states value = PREV }
		}
	}
	natural_disaster_release_state_mission_slots = yes
	natural_disaster_release_special_state_missions = yes
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_state_disruption }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_close_with_partial_strain > 0 } }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_strain }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_strain }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_strain }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_strain }
		set_state_flag = {
			flag = natural_disaster_partial_recovery_strain
			days = @ND_PARTIAL_RECOVERY_STRAIN_DAYS
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_partial_recovery_strain }
	}
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_neighbor_aftermath
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_heat_aftermath
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_terminal_failure_consequence
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_responsibility_transferred
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.closed }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.closed }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.closed }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_cleanup_state = 1 }
	natural_disaster_update_abnormal_history_record = yes
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
	controller = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_closed_abnormal_card > 0 }
				has_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_open
			}
			natural_disaster_rebuild_abnormal_gui_view = yes
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_active_states value = PREV }
		natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = yes
		natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_open_aftermath = yes } }
			remove_ideas = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea
			clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
		}
		natural_disaster_refresh_country_notification_state = yes
		natural_disaster_achievement_finalize_country_recovery = yes
	}
}

# Country scope. Migrates every pending queue row for the transferred state to
# the new responsible country without changing its globally reserved due date.
natural_disaster_transfer_pending_jobs_for_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_index = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index
				value = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index
					value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_type = natural_disaster_job_type_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_sequence_id = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_type_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_delay = natural_disaster_transfer_job_due_date }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_delay = global.date }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_delay < 0 } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_delay = 0 }
			}
			event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country = {
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_type_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_type }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_sequence_id }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_due_date }
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_queue_active
				country_event = {
					id = chaosx.nr13.2
					days = natural_disaster_transfer_job_delay
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_index = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^num < 1 } }
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_queue_active
	}
}

# Country scope. Removes every mission, pointer, and visible-card reference
# held by this country for the state saved as natural_disaster_transfer_state.
natural_disaster_release_transferred_state_pointers = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_transfer_slot
				value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_rescue_cap
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_[SLOT]
						has_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_[SLOT]
						check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_rescue_state_[SLOT] value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state compare = equals }
					}
					remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_[SLOT]
					clear_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_[SLOT]
				}
			}
			SLOT = "[?natural_disaster_transfer_slot|.0]"
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_transfer_slot
				value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_stabilization_cap
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_[SLOT]
						has_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_[SLOT]
						check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_[SLOT] value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state compare = equals }
					}
					remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_[SLOT]
					clear_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_[SLOT]
				}
			}
			SLOT = "[?natural_disaster_transfer_slot|.0]"
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_transfer_slot
				value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_reconstruction_cap
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_[SLOT]
						has_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_[SLOT]
						check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_[SLOT] value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state compare = equals }
					}
					remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_[SLOT]
					clear_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_[SLOT]
				}
			}
			SLOT = "[?natural_disaster_transfer_slot|.0]"
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state
			check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state compare = equals }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission }
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_active
		clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state
			check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state compare = equals }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_mission }
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_secured
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_relief_variant_neighbor_convoy
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_relief_variant_port_lifeline
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_relief_variant_engineer
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_relief_variant_medical
		clear_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state
		clear_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_donor
	}
	remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_active_states value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state }
	remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_abnormal_states value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_active_states^num < 1 }
			NOT = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory }
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
						natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_unread
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
	}
}

# Country scope. Registers the transferred state with its new responsible
# controller and restores only the phase and chain mission that remain live.
natural_disaster_register_transferred_state_responsibility = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state = {
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_responsibility_transferred
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
				}
			}
			event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country = {
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_unread
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes }
			event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country = {
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea } } add_ideas = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = natural_disaster_active_states value = PREV } } }
					add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_active_states value = PREV }
				}
				natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						PREV = {
							check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
							check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_due_date > global.date }
						}
					}
					natural_disaster_activate_family_chain_mission = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_gui_card }
			event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = natural_disaster_abnormal_states value = PREV } } }
					add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_abnormal_states value = PREV }
				}
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_available
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_unread
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_handle_state_control_change = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_new_responsible_country
			OR = {
				is_owned_by = event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country
				is_controlled_by = event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_former_responsible_country }
			event_target:natural_disaster_former_responsible_country = {
				natural_disaster_transfer_pending_jobs_for_state = yes
				natural_disaster_release_transferred_state_pointers = yes
			}
		}
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active
		event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country = { natural_disaster_register_transferred_state_responsibility = yes }
	}
	else = {
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_responsibility_transferred
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_former_responsible_country }
			event_target:natural_disaster_former_responsible_country = { natural_disaster_release_transferred_state_pointers = yes }
		}
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active
		controller = {
			set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
			set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_clear_heat_overlap_for_country = {
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave }
		}
		natural_disaster_close_aftermath_card = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_activate_aftermath_for_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.none } } }
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.active }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none } }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.recovery }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.rescue_days }
		natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
		natural_disaster_apply_state_disruption = yes
		controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_current_state_controller }
		if = {
			limit = { is_controlled_by = event_target:natural_disaster_current_state_controller }
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
			controller = {
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_idea = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea } }
					add_ideas = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							is_in_array = {
								array = natural_disaster_active_states
								value = PREV
							}
						}
					}
					add_to_array = {
						array = natural_disaster_active_states
						value = PREV
					}
				}
				natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
			controller = {
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = {
	set_variable_to_random = {
		var = natural_disaster_report_delay
		min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.report_min_days
		max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.report_max_days
		integer = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.report }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_report_delay }
	natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
}

natural_disaster_schedule_family_news = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.global }
			NOT = {
				meta_trigger = {
					text = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_news_cooldown_[FAMILY_ID] }
					FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.none } }
			NOT = {
				meta_trigger = {
					text = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_news_cooldown_[FAMILY_ID] }
					FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.always } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.major_only }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity > constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.first_family }
				NOT = {
					meta_trigger = {
						text = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_family_seen_[FAMILY_ID] }
						FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.meaningful }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity > constant:natural_disaster_severity.local }
					OWNER = { is_ai = no }
					is_capital = yes
					NOT = {
						meta_trigger = {
							text = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_family_seen_[FAMILY_ID] }
							FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 1 }
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.news }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.minimum_job_delay_days }
		natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				set_global_flag = {
					flag = natural_disaster_news_cooldown_[FAMILY_ID]
					days = [COOLDOWN_DAYS]
				}
			}
			FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
			COOLDOWN_DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_news_cooldown_days|.0]"
		}
	}
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_global_flag = natural_disaster_family_seen_[FAMILY_ID] }
		FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
	}
}

natural_disaster_schedule_chain_followup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
		}
		natural_disaster_achievement_track_chain_schedule = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } }
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_chain_delay
				min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_tsunami_min_days
				max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_tsunami_days
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease } }
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_chain_delay
				min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_disease_min_days
				max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_disease_days
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine } }
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_chain_delay
				min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_famine_min_days
				max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_famine_days
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread } }
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_chain_delay
				min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_wildfire_min_days
				max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_wildfire_days
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse } }
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_chain_delay
				min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_supply_min_days
				max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_supply_days
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } }
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_chain_delay
				min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_lahar_min_days
				max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_lahar_days
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock } }
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_chain_delay
				min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_aftershock_min_days
				max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_aftershock_days
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure } }
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_chain_delay
				min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_refugee_min_days
				max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_refugee_days
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_chain_delay
				min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_political_min_days
				max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_political_days
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.followup }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_chain_delay }
		natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date }
		controller = {
			natural_disaster_activate_family_chain_mission = yes
		}
	}
}

# State scope. Converts a timed objective's full, partial, or failed result
# into separate human, material, and disruption multipliers before the queued
# chain is resolved. Full prevention is handled by clearing the chain itself.
natural_disaster_prepare_chain_outcome_scaling = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.tsunami_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.tsunami_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.tsunami_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.disease_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.disease_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.disease_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine } }
