			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.famine_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.famine_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.famine_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.wildfire_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.wildfire_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.wildfire_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.supply_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.supply_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.supply_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.lahar_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.lahar_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.lahar_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.aftershock_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.aftershock_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.aftershock_partial_disruption }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.failed_death }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.failed_building }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.failed_disruption }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_scale }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_scale }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_scale }
	}
}

natural_disaster_execute_chain_followup = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score > 0 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
			}
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_prevented
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
				OR = {
					has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_1
					has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_2
					has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_3
					has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_4
				}
			}
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
			}
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
			}
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.chained }
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_arrived
			natural_disaster_achievement_track_chain_arrival_before = yes
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_family }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftershock_active
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_aftermath
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_lahar_aftermath
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_wildfire_spread
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_famine_pressure
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_disease_pressure
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_pressure
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_supply_collapse
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.political_shock } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_political_shock
				OWNER = {
					add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_consequence.political_shock_stability
					add_war_support = constant:natural_disaster_consequence.political_shock_war_support
				}
			}

			set_variable = { natural_disaster_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = natural_disaster_severity }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = natural_disaster_evolution }
			natural_disaster_prepare_family_profile = yes
			natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = yes
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.baseline_impact_scale }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.evolution_ii_impact_scale }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 2 } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.evolution_iii_impact_scale }
			}
			natural_disaster_prepare_chain_outcome_scaling = yes
			set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = natural_disaster_death_scale }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = natural_disaster_building_scale }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_original_supply_scale = natural_disaster_supply_scale }
			multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_supply_scale = natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale }
			multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_supply_scale = natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome }

			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
					}
				}
				natural_disaster_apply_population_loss = yes
			}
			natural_disaster_achievement_track_chain_arrival_after = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
					}
				}
				natural_disaster_apply_family_building_damage = yes
			}
			natural_disaster_apply_state_disruption = yes
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_supply_scale = natural_disaster_chain_original_supply_scale }
			natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = yes
			set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 }
					NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending }
				}
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
				set_temp_variable_to_random = {
					var = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_delay
					min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_disease_min_days
					max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_disease_days
					integer = yes
				}
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_arrived
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_success
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score = 0 }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.followup }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_delay }
				natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date }
				controller = { natural_disaster_activate_family_chain_mission = yes }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
				}
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending } }
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_success
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score = 0 }
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_apply_terminal_failure_consequence = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_terminal_failure_consequence } }
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_terminal_failure_consequence
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.terminal_failure_score_penalty }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_last_deaths
					value = constant:natural_disaster_achievement.second_wave_major_death_spike
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.political_shock }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_resources > 0 } }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_shelter > 0 } }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport > 0 } }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.political_shock }
		}
		natural_disaster_apply_state_disruption = yes
		natural_disaster_schedule_chain_followup = yes
		natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_execute_reassessment = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
				NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue } }
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_rescue_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.rescue_success_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_rescue_success
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.recovering }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_rescue_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.rescue_partial_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
				}
				else = {
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
				}
				natural_disaster_release_state_mission_slots = yes
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.middle_stabilization }
			controller = {
				natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
			}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.stabilization_days }
				natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.middle_stabilization } }
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_stabilization_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.stabilization_success_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilization_success
					multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.successful_stabilization_relief }
					multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.successful_stabilization_relief }
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.recovering }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_stabilization_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.stabilization_partial_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial
					multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_stabilization_relief }
					multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_stabilization_relief }
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
				}
				else = {
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
				}
				natural_disaster_release_state_mission_slots = yes
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.late_reconstruction }
			controller = {
				natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
			}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.reconstruction_days }
				natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.late_reconstruction } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.reconstruction_success_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_success
					natural_disaster_close_aftermath_card = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.reconstruction_partial_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
						NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial }
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.recovering }
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.cleanup_reassessment_days }
					natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial }
					natural_disaster_close_aftermath_card = yes
				}
				else = {
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
					natural_disaster_apply_terminal_failure_consequence = yes
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.cleanup_reassessment_days }
					natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.cleanup_reassessment_days }
			natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
		}
		natural_disaster_achievement_track_reassessment = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_prepare_neighbor_profile = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_limit = constant:natural_disaster_damage.abnormal_neighbor_count }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.evolution_iii_neighbor_points }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_limit = constant:natural_disaster_damage.catastrophic_neighbor_count }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.evolution_ii_neighbor_points }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_limit = constant:natural_disaster_damage.regional_neighbor_count }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.evolution_ii_neighbor_points }
	}
}

natural_disaster_apply_neighbor_impact = {
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = nd_parent_sequence_id }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = nd_parent_origin_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_planned_hits = nd_parent_planned_hits }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = natural_disaster_neighbor_severity }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution = natural_disaster_current_evolution }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = nd_parent_report_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = nd_parent_aftermath_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.none }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_neighbor_death_scale = nd_parent_death_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_neighbor_death_scale = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.neighbor_falloff }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_neighbor_building_scale = nd_parent_building_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_neighbor_building_scale = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.neighbor_falloff }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_death_scale = nd_neighbor_death_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_building_scale = nd_neighbor_building_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_scale = nd_parent_recovery_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_country = event_target:nd_parent_caller_country }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.active }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_preparation_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome = 0 }
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_neighbor_aftermath
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_id = nd_parent_sequence_id }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = natural_disaster_neighbor_severity }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = nd_parent_origin_family }
	OWNER = { natural_disaster_achievement_begin_sequence = yes }

	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = natural_disaster_neighbor_severity }
	natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = natural_disaster_neighbor_damage_points }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = 0 }
	natural_disaster_apply_population_loss = yes
	natural_disaster_apply_family_building_damage = yes
	natural_disaster_achievement_track_impact = yes
	natural_disaster_activate_aftermath_for_state = yes
	natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = yes
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
}

natural_disaster_apply_regional_spread = {
	clear_temp_array = natural_disaster_neighbor_candidates
	every_neighbor_state = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_is_valid_impact_state = yes }
		add_to_temp_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_neighbor_candidates
			value = THIS
		}
	}
	natural_disaster_prepare_neighbor_profile = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbors_applied = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbors_applied < natural_disaster_neighbor_limit }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_candidates^num > 0 }
		}
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_neighbor_candidates
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_neighbor_state
			remove_from_temp_array = {
				array = natural_disaster_neighbor_candidates
				value = THIS
			}
			natural_disaster_apply_neighbor_impact = yes
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbors_applied = 1 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_execute_repeated_impact = {
	# A selected-state repeat explicitly supersedes the visible card while
	# retaining its completed work as score. The fresh impact adds burden,
	# damage, deaths, reports, and chains through the same public runtime.
	natural_disaster_achievement_track_card_close = yes
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = natural_disaster_repeat_sequence_id }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_impact_index = natural_disaster_repeat_impact_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_family = natural_disaster_repeat_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = natural_disaster_repeat_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = natural_disaster_repeat_origin_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_planned_hits = natural_disaster_repeat_planned_hits }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_severity > natural_disaster_severity } }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = natural_disaster_repeat_severity }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution = natural_disaster_repeat_evolution }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = natural_disaster_repeat_news_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = natural_disaster_repeat_report_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_repeat_aftermath_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = natural_disaster_repeat_chain_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_death_scale = natural_disaster_repeat_death_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_building_scale = natural_disaster_repeat_building_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_repeat_recovery_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_supply_scale = natural_disaster_repeat_supply_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_country = natural_disaster_repeat_caller_country }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_schedule_date = natural_disaster_repeat_schedule_date }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_segment_index = natural_disaster_repeat_impact_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.impacted }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.none }
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.terminal_failure_score_penalty }
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.terminal_failure_score_penalty }
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.terminal_failure_score_penalty }
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_repeat_impact_scheduled
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.active }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_impact_date = global.date }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_family }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = natural_disaster_severity }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = natural_disaster_evolution }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = natural_disaster_news_policy }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = natural_disaster_report_policy }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_aftermath_policy }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = natural_disaster_chain_policy }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_scale = natural_disaster_death_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_scale = natural_disaster_building_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_news_cooldown_days = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.news_family_cooldown_days }
	natural_disaster_prepare_family_profile = yes
	natural_disaster_prepare_warning_route_profile = yes
	natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = yes
	set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = 0 }
	natural_disaster_apply_population_loss = yes
	natural_disaster_apply_family_building_damage = yes
	natural_disaster_achievement_track_impact = yes
	natural_disaster_resolve_chain_risk = yes
	natural_disaster_apply_state_disruption = yes
	natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = yes
	natural_disaster_schedule_family_news = yes
	natural_disaster_schedule_chain_followup = yes
	natural_disaster_register_abnormal_gui_card = yes
	controller = {
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
		natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_execute_impact = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_repeat_impact_scheduled
				natural_disaster_scheduled_state_is_still_valid = yes
			}
			natural_disaster_execute_repeated_impact = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { natural_disaster_scheduled_state_is_still_valid = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_family }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = natural_disaster_severity }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = natural_disaster_evolution }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_parent_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_parent_origin_family = natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_parent_planned_hits = natural_disaster_sequence_planned_hits }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_parent_report_policy = natural_disaster_report_policy }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_parent_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_aftermath_policy }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_parent_death_scale = natural_disaster_death_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_parent_building_scale = natural_disaster_building_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_parent_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_recovery_scale }
			var:natural_disaster_caller_country = { save_event_target_as = nd_parent_caller_country }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = natural_disaster_news_policy }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = natural_disaster_report_policy }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_aftermath_policy }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = natural_disaster_chain_policy }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_scale = natural_disaster_death_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_scale = natural_disaster_building_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_recovery_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_news_cooldown_days = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.news_family_cooldown_days }
			natural_disaster_prepare_family_profile = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_warning.timely_outcome }
			}
			natural_disaster_prepare_warning_route_profile = yes
			natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = yes
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.active }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_impact_date = global.date }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.impacted }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = 0 }
			natural_disaster_apply_population_loss = yes
			natural_disaster_apply_family_building_damage = yes
			natural_disaster_achievement_track_impact = yes
			natural_disaster_resolve_chain_risk = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none } }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.chain_risk }
			}
			natural_disaster_activate_aftermath_for_state = yes
			natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = yes
			natural_disaster_schedule_family_news = yes
			natural_disaster_schedule_chain_followup = yes

			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 1 }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity > constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { nd_spread_parent_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
				set_temp_variable = { nd_spread_parent_severity = natural_disaster_current_severity }
				set_temp_variable = { nd_spread_parent_evolution = natural_disaster_current_evolution }
				natural_disaster_apply_regional_spread = yes
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = nd_spread_parent_family }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = nd_spread_parent_severity }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = nd_spread_parent_evolution }
			}
			natural_disaster_maybe_emit_family_super_event = yes

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_heat_aftermath
			}
		}
		else = {
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.expired }
		}
	}
}

# ============================================================================
# ACHIEVEMENT TRACKING
# Country attempts bind to Event 013 sequence ids. State guards prevent a card
# from contributing twice while allowing later sequences to revisit a state.
# ============================================================================

natural_disaster_achievement_begin_sequence = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_call_severity
				value = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_season_started }
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_recovery_closed
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_recovery_deadline_failed
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_aftermath_abandoned
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_season_ended
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_recovery_closed
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_recovery_deadline_failed
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_aftermath_abandoned
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_season_started
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_sequence_id = nd_achievement_sequence_id }
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_cards_expected = 0 }
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_cards_recovered = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_meteor_shower_started }
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_meteor_shower_ended
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_capital_failed
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_supply_route_failed
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_airfield_network_failed
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_meteor_shower_ended
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_capital_operational
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_supply_route_operational
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_airfield_network_operational
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_capital_failed
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_supply_route_failed
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_airfield_network_failed
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_meteor_shower_started
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_skyfall_sequence_id = nd_achievement_sequence_id }
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_skyfall_survived_hits = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_recovery_started }
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_recovery_closed
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_attempt_failed
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_aftershock_matured
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_tsunami_matured
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_recovery_closed
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_ended
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_attempt_failed
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_aftershock_matured
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_tsunami_matured
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_recovery_started
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_id = nd_achievement_sequence_id }
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_rupture_regions_recovered = 0 }
	}
	natural_disaster_achievement_check_operational_state = yes
}

natural_disaster_achievement_begin_transport_attempt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_recovery_started }
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_recovery_complete
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_attempt_failed
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_capital_supply_route_failed
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_recovery_complete
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_sequence_ended
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_attempt_failed
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_capital_supply_route_failed
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_recovery_started
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_sequence_id = nd_achievement_impact_sequence }
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_cards_expected = 0 }
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_cards_recovered = 0 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_begin_ashfall_attempt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_major_ashfall_started }
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_recovery_complete
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_attempt_failed
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_ash_famine_matured
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_recovery_complete
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_ended
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_attempt_failed
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_ash_famine_matured
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_major_ashfall_started
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_id = nd_achievement_impact_sequence }
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_states_affected = 0 }
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_states_recovered = 0 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_track_impact = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_sequence = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_family = natural_disaster_family }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_origin = natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_severity = natural_disaster_severity }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_transport_damage = natural_disaster_damage_transport }

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = nd_achievement_impact_severity
				value = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_transport_damage > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { natural_disaster_achievement_begin_transport_attempt = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
			check_variable = {
				var = nd_achievement_impact_severity
				value = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		OWNER = { natural_disaster_achievement_begin_ashfall_attempt = yes }
	}

	OWNER = {
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_severe_sequence = achievement_nd_severe_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_transport_sequence = achievement_nd_transport_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_ash_sequence = achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_skyfall_sequence = achievement_nd_skyfall_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_rupture_sequence = achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_barrage_sequence = achievement_nd_barrage_sequence_id }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = nd_achievement_impact_severity
				value = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_sequence = nd_achievement_severe_sequence }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					var = achievement_nd_severe_counted_sequence
					value = nd_achievement_impact_sequence
					compare = equals
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_counted_sequence = nd_achievement_impact_sequence }
		OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_cards_expected = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_sequence = nd_achievement_transport_sequence }
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_transport_damage > 0 }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					var = achievement_nd_transport_counted_sequence
					value = nd_achievement_impact_sequence
					compare = equals
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_counted_sequence = nd_achievement_impact_sequence }
		OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_cards_expected = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_sequence = nd_achievement_ash_sequence }
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					var = achievement_nd_ashfall_counted_sequence
					value = nd_achievement_impact_sequence
					compare = equals
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_counted_sequence = nd_achievement_impact_sequence }
		OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_states_affected = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_sequence = nd_achievement_skyfall_sequence }
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_impact_origin = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					var = achievement_nd_skyfall_counted_sequence
					value = nd_achievement_impact_sequence
					compare = equals
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_skyfall_counted_sequence = nd_achievement_impact_sequence }
		OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_skyfall_survived_hits = 1 } }
	}
	OWNER = { natural_disaster_achievement_check_operational_state = yes }
}

natural_disaster_achievement_record_report_group = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_report_group_ground
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_report_group_water
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_report_group_weather
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm }
				check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_report_group_climate
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_track_chain_schedule = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
			}
		}
		set_state_flag = achievement_nd_tracked_tsunami_risk
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_tsunami_card_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
		OWNER = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_tsunami_prevention_started }
						has_country_flag = achievement_nd_tsunami_chain_prevented
						has_country_flag = achievement_nd_second_wave_arrived
					}
				}
				clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_tsunami_chain_prevented
				clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_second_wave_arrived
				clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_second_wave_death_spike
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_tsunami_prevention_started
				set_variable = { achievement_nd_tsunami_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
				set_variable = { achievement_nd_tsunami_preventions = 0 }
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_record_chain_prevention = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = achievement_nd_tracked_tsunami_risk
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_tsunami_card_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
			OWNER = {
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_tsunami_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			}
		}
		OWNER = {
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_tsunami_chain_prevented
			add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_tsunami_preventions = 1 }
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_track_chain_arrival_before = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = natural_disaster_chain_risk }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_origin = natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = achievement_nd_tracked_tsunami_risk
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_tsunami_card_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
			OWNER = {
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_tsunami_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_second_wave_arrived }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_origin = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
			OWNER = {
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_aftershock_matured }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_origin = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
			OWNER = {
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_tsunami_matured }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine }
			OWNER = {
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_ash_famine_matured }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_severity
				value = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		OWNER = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_protection_started }
						has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_pressure_resolved
						has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_attempt_failed
						has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_death_threshold_failed
						has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_camp_disease_failed
						has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_border_camp_failed
					}
				}
				clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_pressure_resolved
				clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_ended
				clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_attempt_failed
				clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_death_threshold_failed
				clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_camp_disease_failed
				clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_border_camp_failed
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_protection_started
				set_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
				set_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_routes_completed = 0 }
				set_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_cards_expected = 0 }
				set_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_cards_recovered = 0 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OWNER = {
					check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
				}
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = achievement_nd_refugee_counted_sequence
						value = natural_disaster_sequence_id
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_1
			clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_2
			clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_3
			clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_4
			set_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_card
			set_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_counted_sequence = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_cards_expected = 1 } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_card
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_counted_sequence = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
			OWNER = {
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_camp_disease_failed }
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_track_chain_arrival_after = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = achievement_nd_tracked_tsunami_risk
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_tsunami_card_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
			OWNER = {
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_tsunami_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_last_deaths
				value = constant:natural_disaster_achievement.second_wave_major_death_spike
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_second_wave_death_spike }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_card
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_counted_sequence = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			OWNER = {
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_last_deaths
				value = constant:natural_disaster_achievement.refugee_death_failure_threshold
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_death_threshold_failed }
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_track_reassessment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.late_reconstruction }
		}
		OWNER = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_season_started
					check_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
				}
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_recovery_deadline_failed
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_card
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_counted_sequence = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
			OWNER = {
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_border_camp_failed }
	}
	OWNER = { natural_disaster_achievement_check_operational_state = yes }
}

natural_disaster_achievement_record_catalogue_group = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_catalogue_seismic_recovered }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_catalogue_water_coastal_recovered }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_catalogue_fire_drought_recovered }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_catalogue_winter_recovered }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_catalogue_storm_recovered }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_catalogue_mass_movement_recovered }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
			}
		}
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_catalogue_volcanic_ash_recovered }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_catalogue_dust_sand_recovered }
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_track_card_close = {
	OWNER = {
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_severe_sequence = achievement_nd_severe_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_transport_sequence = achievement_nd_transport_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_ash_sequence = achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_skyfall_sequence = achievement_nd_skyfall_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_rupture_sequence = achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_barrage_sequence = achievement_nd_barrage_sequence_id }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_success }
		natural_disaster_achievement_record_catalogue_group = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = nd_achievement_severe_sequence }
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_counted_sequence = nd_achievement_severe_sequence }
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = achievement_nd_severe_recovered_sequence
						value = nd_achievement_severe_sequence
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_recovered_sequence = nd_achievement_severe_sequence }
			OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_cards_recovered = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = nd_achievement_transport_sequence }
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_counted_sequence = nd_achievement_transport_sequence }
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = achievement_nd_transport_recovered_sequence
						value = nd_achievement_transport_sequence
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_recovered_sequence = nd_achievement_transport_sequence }
			OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_cards_recovered = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = nd_achievement_ash_sequence }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = achievement_nd_ashfall_recovered_sequence
						value = nd_achievement_ash_sequence
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_recovered_sequence = nd_achievement_ash_sequence }
			OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_states_recovered = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = nd_achievement_rupture_sequence }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = achievement_nd_rupture_recovered_sequence
						value = nd_achievement_rupture_sequence
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = { achievement_nd_rupture_recovered_sequence = nd_achievement_rupture_sequence }
			OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_rupture_regions_recovered = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = nd_achievement_barrage_sequence }
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = achievement_nd_barrage_recovered_sequence
						value = nd_achievement_barrage_sequence
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = { achievement_nd_barrage_recovered_sequence = nd_achievement_barrage_sequence }
			OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_barrage_recovery_cards = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_card
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_counted_sequence = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
				OWNER = {
					check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
				}
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = achievement_nd_refugee_recovered_sequence
						value = natural_disaster_sequence_id
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_recovered_sequence = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_cards_recovered = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial }
		OWNER = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_aftermath_abandoned
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_attempt_failed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_attempt_failed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_attempt_failed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_attempt_failed
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		# A forced or invalid-target close is an abandoned recovery, never a
		# silent success. Mark every attempt owned by this exact sequence as
		# failed so a later qualifying season can start cleanly.
		OWNER = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_aftermath_abandoned
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_attempt_failed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_attempt_failed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_attempt_failed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_attempt_failed
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_finalize_country_recovery = {
	# Sample physical routes before any completion flag freezes an attempt.
	natural_disaster_achievement_check_operational_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_season_started
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_season_ended
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_cards_expected > 0 }
			check_variable = {
				var = achievement_nd_severe_cards_recovered
				value = achievement_nd_severe_cards_expected
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_open_aftermath = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_recovery_closed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_recovery_started
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_sequence_ended
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_attempt_failed }
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_cards_expected > 0 }
			check_variable = {
				var = achievement_nd_transport_cards_recovered
				value = achievement_nd_transport_cards_expected
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_recovery_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_sequence_ended
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_recovery_complete }
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_transport_cards_expected > 0 }
			check_variable = {
				var = achievement_nd_transport_cards_recovered
				value = achievement_nd_transport_cards_expected
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_attempt_failed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_major_ashfall_started
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_ended
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_attempt_failed }
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_states_affected > 0 }
			check_variable = {
				var = achievement_nd_ashfall_states_recovered
				value = achievement_nd_ashfall_states_affected
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_recovery_complete
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_ended
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_recovery_complete }
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_ashfall_states_affected > 0 }
			check_variable = {
				var = achievement_nd_ashfall_states_recovered
				value = achievement_nd_ashfall_states_affected
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_attempt_failed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_recovery_started
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_ended
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_attempt_failed }
			check_variable = {
				var = achievement_nd_rupture_regions_recovered
				value = constant:natural_disaster_achievement.aftershock_successes
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_recovery_closed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_ended
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_recovery_closed }
			check_variable = {
				var = achievement_nd_rupture_regions_recovered
				value = constant:natural_disaster_achievement.aftershock_successes
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_attempt_failed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_protection_started
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_ended
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_attempt_failed }
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_cards_expected > 0 }
			check_variable = {
				var = achievement_nd_refugee_cards_recovered
				value = achievement_nd_refugee_cards_expected
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_routes_completed > 0 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_death_threshold_failed }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_camp_disease_failed }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_border_camp_failed }
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_pressure_resolved
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_ended
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_pressure_resolved }
			OR = {
				check_variable = {
					var = achievement_nd_refugee_cards_recovered
					value = achievement_nd_refugee_cards_expected
					compare = less_than
				}
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_routes_completed < 1 }
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_death_threshold_failed
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_camp_disease_failed
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_border_camp_failed
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_attempt_failed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_started
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_impact_sequence_ended
			check_variable = {
				var = achievement_nd_barrage_recovery_cards
				value = constant:natural_disaster_achievement.barrage_maximum_recovery_cards
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_capital_route_preserved
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_port_corridor_preserved
				has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_rail_corridor_preserved
			}
			NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_open_aftermath = yes }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_country_collapsed }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_aftermath_unresolved }
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_ended
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_check_operational_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_meteor_shower_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_meteor_shower_ended }
		}
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_capital_operational
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_supply_route_operational
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_airfield_network_operational
		set_temp_variable = { nd_operational_airfield_states = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_controlled_by = PREV
				air_base > 0
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { nd_operational_airfield_states = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { natural_disaster_country_has_operational_capital_route = yes }
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_capital_operational
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_capital_failed }
		if = {
			limit = { natural_disaster_country_has_operational_supply_route = yes }
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_supply_route_operational
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_supply_route_failed }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = nd_operational_airfield_states
					value = constant:natural_disaster_achievement.operational_airfield_states
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_airfield_network_operational
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = achievement_nd_skyfall_airfield_network_failed }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_recovery_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_sequence_ended }
			OR = {
				NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_operational_capital_route = yes }
				NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_operational_supply_route = yes }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_capital_supply_route_failed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_ended }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_aftermath_unresolved }
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_capital_route_preserved
			NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_operational_capital_route = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_capital_route_preserved
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_ended }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_aftermath_unresolved }
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_port_corridor_preserved
			NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_operational_port_corridor = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_port_corridor_preserved
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_ended }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_aftermath_unresolved }
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_rail_corridor_preserved
			NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_operational_rail_corridor = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_rail_corridor_preserved
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_initialize_barrage_routes = {
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_capital_route_preserved
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_port_corridor_preserved
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_rail_corridor_preserved
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_country_has_operational_capital_route = yes }
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_capital_route_preserved
	}
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_country_has_operational_port_corridor = yes }
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_port_corridor_preserved
	}
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_country_has_operational_rail_corridor = yes }
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_rail_corridor_preserved
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_check_sequence_pending = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_pending = 0 }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries^num
		value = nd_achievement_job_index
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries^nd_achievement_job_index
					value = nd_achievement_sequence_to_check
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_pending = 1 }
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_mark_sequence_ended = {
	# The last queued job may itself damage rails, ports, infrastructure, or
	# airfields. Capture the terminal physical state before setting end flags.
	natural_disaster_achievement_check_operational_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_season_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_season_ended }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_to_check = achievement_nd_severe_sequence_id }
		natural_disaster_achievement_check_sequence_pending = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_pending < 1 } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_season_ended
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { achievement_nd_severe_cards_expected < 1 } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_severe_aftermath_abandoned
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_recovery_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_sequence_ended }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_to_check = achievement_nd_transport_sequence_id }
		natural_disaster_achievement_check_sequence_pending = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_pending < 1 } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_transport_sequence_ended
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_major_ashfall_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_ended }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_to_check = achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_id }
		natural_disaster_achievement_check_sequence_pending = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_pending < 1 } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_ashfall_sequence_ended
		}
	}
	if = {
