		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_meteor_shower_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_meteor_shower_ended }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_to_check = achievement_nd_skyfall_sequence_id }
		natural_disaster_achievement_check_sequence_pending = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_pending < 1 } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_meteor_shower_ended
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_recovery_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_ended }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_to_check = achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_id }
		natural_disaster_achievement_check_sequence_pending = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_pending < 1 } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_rupture_sequence_ended
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_protection_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_ended }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_to_check = achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_id }
		natural_disaster_achievement_check_sequence_pending = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_pending < 1 } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_ended
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_impact_sequence_ended }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_to_check = achievement_nd_barrage_sequence_id }
		natural_disaster_achievement_check_sequence_pending = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_pending < 1 } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_impact_sequence_ended
		}
	}
	natural_disaster_achievement_finalize_country_recovery = yes
}

natural_disaster_achievement_begin_maximum_barrage = {
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_ended
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_impact_sequence_ended
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_deadline_reached
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_country_collapsed
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_aftermath_unresolved
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_capital_route_preserved
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_port_corridor_preserved
	clr_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_rail_corridor_preserved
	set_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_started
	set_variable = { achievement_nd_barrage_sequence_id = natural_disaster_last_sequence_id }
	set_variable = { achievement_nd_barrage_recovery_cards = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = achievement_nd_barrage_attempt_id } }
		set_variable = { achievement_nd_barrage_attempt_id = 0 }
	}
	add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_barrage_attempt_id = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_barrage_expiry_days = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.season_expiry_days }
	set_variable = { achievement_nd_barrage_expiry_date = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_barrage_expiry_date = nd_barrage_expiry_days }
	country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.3 days = nd_barrage_expiry_days }
	natural_disaster_achievement_initialize_barrage_routes = yes
	natural_disaster_achievement_check_operational_state = yes
}

natural_disaster_achievement_on_capitulation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_ended }
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_country_collapsed
	}
}

natural_disaster_achievement_record_refugee_action = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_card
			check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_counted_sequence = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			check_variable = { nd_refugee_action_id > 0 }
			OWNER = {
				check_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_sequence_id = PREV.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			}
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_[ACTION_ID] } }
					set_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_[ACTION_ID]
					OWNER = { add_to_variable = { achievement_nd_refugee_routes_completed = 1 } }
				}
			}
			ACTION_ID = "[?nd_refugee_action_id|.0]"
		}
	}
}

# ============================================================================
# EVOLUTION III ABNORMAL PATH MAP
# ============================================================================

natural_disaster_prepare_abnormal_history_record = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_family = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_origin_family = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_severity = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_sequence_id = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_segment_index = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.closed }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_schedule_date = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_reassessment_date = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_deaths = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_warning_protection = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_warning_outcome = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.closed }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.closed }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_rescue_score = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_stabilization_score = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_reconstruction_score = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_chain_risk = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_damage_transport = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_damage_industry = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_damage_ports = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_damage_air = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_damage_resources = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_linked_state = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_has_linked_state = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_record_status = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.history }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_record_result = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_result.normal }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_relief_status = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_family }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_family = natural_disaster_family }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_origin_family = natural_disaster_family }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_origin_family = natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_severity }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_severity = natural_disaster_severity }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_path_segment_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_segment_index = natural_disaster_path_segment_index }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_path_status }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_path_status = natural_disaster_path_status }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_schedule_date }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_schedule_date = natural_disaster_schedule_date }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_next_reassessment }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_reassessment_date = natural_disaster_next_reassessment }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_last_deaths }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_deaths = natural_disaster_last_deaths }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_warning_protection }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_warning_protection = natural_disaster_warning_protection }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_warning_outcome }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_warning_outcome = natural_disaster_warning_outcome }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_card_state }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_card_state = natural_disaster_card_state }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_phase }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_phase = natural_disaster_phase }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_score }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_rescue_score = natural_disaster_rescue_score }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_score }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_stabilization_score = natural_disaster_stabilization_score }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_score }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_reconstruction_score = natural_disaster_reconstruction_score }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_chain_risk }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_chain_risk = natural_disaster_chain_risk }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_transport }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_damage_transport = natural_disaster_damage_transport }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_industry }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_damage_industry = natural_disaster_damage_industry }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_ports }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_damage_ports = natural_disaster_damage_ports }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_air }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_damage_air = natural_disaster_damage_air }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_resources }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_damage_resources = natural_disaster_damage_resources }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_linked_state }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_linked_state = natural_disaster_linked_state }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_has_linked_state = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_record_status = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.impact_scheduled }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_record_status = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.warning_active }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_record_status = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.warning_scheduled }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_record_status = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.recovery }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_record_result = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_result.partial }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_record_result = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_result.failed }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_record_result = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_result.unresolved }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_history_relief_status = 1 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_append_abnormal_history_record = {
	natural_disaster_prepare_abnormal_history_record = yes
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_state_entries^num }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_abnormal_history_registered_sequence_id = natural_disaster_history_sequence_id }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_state_entries value = THIS }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_family_entries value = natural_disaster_history_family }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_origin_family_entries value = natural_disaster_history_origin_family }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_severity_entries value = natural_disaster_history_severity }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_sequence_id_entries value = natural_disaster_history_sequence_id }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_segment_entries value = natural_disaster_history_segment_index }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_path_status_entries value = natural_disaster_history_path_status }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_schedule_date_entries value = natural_disaster_history_schedule_date }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_reassessment_date_entries value = natural_disaster_history_reassessment_date }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_deaths_entries value = natural_disaster_history_deaths }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_warning_protection_entries value = natural_disaster_history_warning_protection }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_warning_outcome_entries value = natural_disaster_history_warning_outcome }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_card_state_entries value = natural_disaster_history_card_state }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_phase_entries value = natural_disaster_history_phase }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_rescue_score_entries value = natural_disaster_history_rescue_score }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_stabilization_score_entries value = natural_disaster_history_stabilization_score }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_reconstruction_score_entries value = natural_disaster_history_reconstruction_score }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_chain_risk_entries value = natural_disaster_history_chain_risk }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_transport_entries value = natural_disaster_history_damage_transport }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_industry_entries value = natural_disaster_history_damage_industry }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_ports_entries value = natural_disaster_history_damage_ports }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_air_entries value = natural_disaster_history_damage_air }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_resources_entries value = natural_disaster_history_damage_resources }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_linked_state_entries value = natural_disaster_history_linked_state }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_has_linked_state_entries value = natural_disaster_history_has_linked_state }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries value = natural_disaster_history_record_status }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_result_entries value = natural_disaster_history_record_result }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_relief_status_entries value = natural_disaster_history_relief_status }
	add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_excluded_rebuild_entries value = 0 }
}

natural_disaster_update_abnormal_history_record = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index
			has_variable = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_registered_sequence_id
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_registered_sequence_id
				value = natural_disaster_sequence_id
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		natural_disaster_prepare_abnormal_history_record = yes
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_family_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_family }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_origin_family_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_origin_family }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_severity_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_severity }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_sequence_id_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_sequence_id }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_segment_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_segment_index }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_path_status_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_path_status }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_schedule_date_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_schedule_date }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_reassessment_date_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_reassessment_date }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_deaths_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_deaths }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_warning_protection_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_warning_protection }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_warning_outcome_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_warning_outcome }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_card_state_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_card_state }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_phase_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_phase }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_rescue_score_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_rescue_score }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_stabilization_score_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_stabilization_score }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_reconstruction_score_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_reconstruction_score }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_chain_risk_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_chain_risk }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_transport_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_damage_transport }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_industry_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_damage_industry }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_ports_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_damage_ports }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_air_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_damage_air }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_resources_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_damage_resources }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_linked_state_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_linked_state }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_has_linked_state_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_has_linked_state }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_record_status }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_result_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_record_result }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_relief_status_entries^natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index = natural_disaster_history_relief_status }
	}
}

natural_disaster_register_abnormal_gui_card = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.abnormal_path }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_gui_card
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_card
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_registered_sequence_id
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_registered_sequence_id
					value = natural_disaster_sequence_id
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			natural_disaster_update_abnormal_history_record = yes
		}
		else = { natural_disaster_append_abnormal_history_record = yes }
		controller = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = natural_disaster_abnormal_states
							value = PREV
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_array = {
					array = natural_disaster_abnormal_states
					value = PREV
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_available
			set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_unread
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					NOT = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_notice_cooldown }
				}
				set_country_flag = {
					flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_notice_cooldown
					days = @ND_ABNORMAL_NOTICE_COOLDOWN_DAYS
				}
				PREV = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_abnormal_notice_state }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.4 }
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_prepare_gui_urgency_score = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency = constant:natural_disaster_gui.urgency_history }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.impact_scheduled
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency = constant:natural_disaster_gui.urgency_impact_pending }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.warning_active
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency = constant:natural_disaster_gui.urgency_warning_active }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.warning_scheduled
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency = constant:natural_disaster_gui.urgency_warning_scheduled }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.recovery
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency = constant:natural_disaster_gui.urgency_recovery }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.recovery
			}
			check_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_chain_risk_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency = constant:natural_disaster_gui.urgency_chain_risk }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_severity_urgency = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_severity_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_severity_urgency = constant:natural_disaster_gui.urgency_severity_weight }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency = natural_disaster_gui_severity_urgency }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_due_date = global.date }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_due_date = constant:natural_disaster_gui.urgency_date_horizon_days }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_has_live_date = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = {
					global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.impact_scheduled
				}
				check_variable = {
					global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.warning_scheduled
				}
				check_variable = {
					global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.warning_active
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_due_date = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_schedule_date_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_has_live_date = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index = constant:natural_disaster_gui_record_status.recovery
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_due_date = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_reassessment_date_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_has_live_date = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_has_live_date > 0 } }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_due_date = global.date }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = natural_disaster_gui_due_date
			min = 0
			max = constant:natural_disaster_gui.urgency_date_horizon_days
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_date_urgency = constant:natural_disaster_gui.urgency_date_horizon_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_date_urgency = natural_disaster_gui_due_date }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency = natural_disaster_gui_date_urgency }
	}
}

natural_disaster_rebuild_abnormal_gui_view = {
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_state_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_record_index_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_family_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_origin_family_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_severity_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_sequence_id_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_segment_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_path_status_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_schedule_date_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_reassessment_date_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_deaths_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_warning_protection_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_warning_outcome_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_card_state_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_phase_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_rescue_score_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_stabilization_score_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_reconstruction_score_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_chain_risk_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_damage_transport_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_damage_industry_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_damage_ports_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_damage_air_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_damage_resources_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_linked_state_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_has_linked_state_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_record_status_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_record_result_entries
	clear_array = natural_disaster_gui_relief_status_entries
	clear_temp_array = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_indices
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.natural_disaster_gui_rebuild_id } }
		set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_gui_rebuild_id = 0 }
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.natural_disaster_gui_rebuild_id = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_current_rebuild_id = global.natural_disaster_gui_rebuild_id }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_view }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_state_entries^num
			value = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_index
			var:global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_state_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_gui_card
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index
							value = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_index
							compare = equals
						}
					}
					natural_disaster_update_abnormal_history_record = yes
				}
			}
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_state_entries^num
			value = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_index
			add_to_temp_array = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_indices = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_index }
		}
	}
	else = {
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = natural_disaster_abnormal_states^num
			value = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_index
			var:natural_disaster_abnormal_states^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_gui_card
						OR = {
							has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
							has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
							has_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
							natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
						}
					}
					natural_disaster_update_abnormal_history_record = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index
							has_variable = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_registered_sequence_id
							check_variable = {
								var = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_registered_sequence_id
								value = natural_disaster_sequence_id
								compare = equals
							}
						}
						add_to_temp_array = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_indices = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_index }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_route_segments_total = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_indices^num }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_selected_sequence_id = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_slot = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_slot < constant:natural_disaster_gui.card_limit } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_best_urgency = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_best_segment = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.path_sort_sentinel }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_found = 0 }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_indices^num
			value = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_index
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_indices^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_index }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						check_variable = {
							var = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_excluded_rebuild_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index
							value = natural_disaster_gui_current_rebuild_id
							compare = equals
						}
					}
				}
				natural_disaster_prepare_gui_urgency_score = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency > natural_disaster_gui_best_urgency }
							AND = {
								check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency value = natural_disaster_gui_best_urgency compare = equals }
								check_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_segment_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index < natural_disaster_gui_best_segment }
							}
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_best_urgency = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_urgency }
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_best_segment = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_segment_entries^natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index }
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index = natural_disaster_gui_candidate_record_index }
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_found = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_found > 0 } }
			set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_excluded_rebuild_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index = natural_disaster_gui_current_rebuild_id }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_slot = 0 } }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_selected_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_sequence_id_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			}
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_record_index_entries value = natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_state_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_state_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_family_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_family_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_origin_family_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_origin_family_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_severity_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_severity_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_sequence_id_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_sequence_id_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_segment_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_segment_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_path_status_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_path_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_schedule_date_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_schedule_date_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_reassessment_date_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_reassessment_date_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_deaths_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_deaths_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_warning_protection_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_warning_protection_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_warning_outcome_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_warning_outcome_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_card_state_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_card_state_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_phase_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_phase_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_rescue_score_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_rescue_score_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_stabilization_score_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_stabilization_score_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_reconstruction_score_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_reconstruction_score_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_chain_risk_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_chain_risk_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_damage_transport_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_transport_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_damage_industry_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_industry_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_damage_ports_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_ports_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_damage_air_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_air_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_damage_resources_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_damage_resources_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_linked_state_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_linked_state_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_has_linked_state_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_has_linked_state_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_record_status_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_record_result_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_record_result_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_gui_relief_status_entries value = global.natural_disaster_abnormal_history_relief_status_entries^natural_disaster_gui_best_record_index }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_slot = constant:natural_disaster_gui.card_limit }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_slot = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_state_entries^num < 1 } }
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_dormant
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_view } }
			clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_available
		}
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_dormant
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_view } }
			set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_available
		}
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_route_segments_remaining = natural_disaster_gui_route_segments_total }
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_route_segments_remaining = natural_disaster_gui_state_entries^num }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_gui_selected_index }
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_gui_selected_index value = natural_disaster_gui_state_entries^num compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_selected_index = 0 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_open_abnormal_gui = {
	clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_view
	natural_disaster_rebuild_abnormal_gui_view = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_gui_state_entries^num > 0 } }
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_open
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_unread
	}
}

natural_disaster_open_abnormal_gui_history = {
	set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_history_view
	natural_disaster_rebuild_abnormal_gui_view = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_evolution_iii_logged }
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_open
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_unread
	}
}
